Sales — detailed edge-case test book

266 test cases in three parts, with the exact figures to type and what each screen must show. Written for the test copy (opstest) with dummy data only, update 9999868, 19 Nov 2026.

How to use this book

1. Use the test copy only, never the live site. 2. Each case says who to log in as, what to set up before you start, then each step: Do this → You should see. Tick ☐ when it matches. 3. Where a case shows a coloured box, it found a problem: green fixed in this update (check it now works), blue fixed earlier, amber waiting for your decision, red not fixed yet. In a case with a green box, a step that says “Seen: …” describes the old, wrong behaviour; what you should see now is the “Correct: …” part (or the plain rule in the green box). 4. Dates in the cases are worked out for Thursday 19 Nov 2026; on another day count the same way. 5. No passwords are written here; use the test logins you already have.

Questions only you can answer (30)

Nothing was changed for these. Each says what it affects.

CaseQuestion and what it affects
EC-SALC-056Should "Received this month" be limited to the person's offices (like Overdue) instead of every office? It is labelled "all customers".
EC-SALC-069(1) Should the Marketing Manager (head of sales, Level 2 approver) set targets and see the team on Today/Dashboard? (2) Should a Branch Manager set targets only for salespeople of their own branch?
EC-SALC-080How should an unregistered client (no GSTIN) get its state for GST — from its billing address, or a State box on the registration form?
EC-SALC-089Client codes use the calendar year (AHM-27 from 1 Jan 2027) while deal and invoice numbers use the financial year. Is that intended?
EC-SALC-102Excel import and Connect registration always number a firm under the head office (AHM-…) even when it belongs to another branch. Should the import use the row's Home branch?
EC-SALA-018C14 is open: refuse a negative deal value? Proposed yes. Impact: pipeline and forecast understate by the negative amounts.
EC-SALA-029C1 (blocked customer: nothing at all, or business up to acceptance with a banner) is still open.
EC-SALA-032C3 is open: should the Business Director delete deals and enquiries by default? Proposed: no.
EC-SALA-043Should "Queue every lead" be allowed before a connection exists? Today it queues 2 leads although the page says nothing about Ads Pro happens until connected.
EC-SALA-048On the live site (MySQL) a 500-character subject goes into a shorter column — please check once on opstest that it is saved (not an error page). Not testable on this copy (SQLite).
EC-SALA-053C3 (Director deleting enquiries and deals) is open.
EC-SALA-067Should a target margin above a sensible ceiling (say 60%) ask "Are you sure?" Proposed: a warning only (no rule exists today).
EC-SALA-071A15 (refuse negative heads) and C14 (refuse negative costing percentages) are awaiting the owner's yes.
EC-SALA-078Should a conversation be loggable on a closed (won/lost) deal or lead? Proposed: yes (after-sales notes), but it should not set a new follow-up on a closed record. Impact: Today could list follow-ups on lost deals.
EC-SALA-081May the number of a deleted lead or deal be given to the next new one? Proposed: no — numbers run on, a gap is fine. Impact: the deleted record's History and e-mails would point at a different record with the same number.
EC-SALB-017Are quantities with 3 decimals needed on quotations (Sales pack A8 had them on invoices)? Impact: 0.125 month cannot be quoted; it must be 0.12 or 0.13 (₹14,400 or ₹15,600 instead of ₹15,000).
EC-SALB-029Taking an approval back accepts an empty reason. Should a reason be required, as for rejecting? Impact: the history line says only “taken back by Test BranchMgr”.
EC-SALB-032When the levels leave a gap, should the screen refuse the gap (as it refuses overlaps), or is “highest level + lower levels passed” right? Impact: a ₹4,50,000 quotation waits for the Business Director.
EC-SALB-034Should removing a level warn “1 quotation is waiting at this level”? Impact: today it is removed without a word and the waiting step keeps its old approver.
EC-SALB-035C9 is still open: refuse level 0 and negative levels with their own sentence? Impact: today the message talks about level 1 when the person typed 0.
EC-SALB-039C12 (settings saved empty say “saved”) is still open for this screen.
EC-SALB-040Before the quotation is submitted, Test BranchMgr pressing Waive gets “You cannot update this quote.” (not the waiver sentence). Is that the intended wording?
EC-SALB-042Should “Send to client” be refused when there is no customer e-mail (pointing to “✓ I have sent it — mark sent” instead)? Impact: a quotation is recorded as sent and starts to expire without reaching anyone.
EC-SALB-050C7 still open: refuse “Other (specify)” without text?
EC-SALB-063The generator takes the lowest free number, so a deleted AHM/C/2026-27/00001 would be issued again to the next contract. Is re-using a number acceptable?
EC-SALB-068For Ahmedabad contracts the list of final approvers names Test BranchMgr2 (Mumbai) too — that is owner's open item A1 / C2 (cross-branch signers).
EC-SALB-070Is a tolerance of 0.001% intended (99.9999% passes as 100%)? Impact: none on money (the last milestone absorbs it), but the shares shown do not add to 100.
EC-SALB-071Is a retention of 99.999% sensible, or should there be a ceiling (e.g. 20%)? Impact: “Payable now” on each milestone bill would be almost nothing.
EC-SALB-076An external quotation stores the typed total as its value before GST (GST 0%). If the portal total included GST, the contract and the approval band use the GST-inclusive figure. Should the external form ask for the value before GST?
EC-SALB-081Should a group contract left without a value take the quotation's value, or be refused? Impact: a ₹0 contract shows nothing to bill against or to utilise.

Part 1 — Simple Sales: Sales home, Today, Pipeline board, new lead, the one log form, orders, renewals, targets, Sales settings, one firm record — 102 cases

EC-SALC-001NUMBERS

Set-up for this part, and the Sales home cards worked out by hand

Who: Test BDM (login test.bdm) · Screen: /sales-home · Buttons: Sales home tile

Why it matters: Sales home is the first screen a salesperson sees each morning; if a card is off by one lead or one lakh, every other Sales screen is doubted.

Before you start: Create, as Test BDM (Ahmedabad), with Sales → New lead or the full lead form: EC Overdue Co (mobile 98250 11111, value ₹2,00,000, next follow-up 16 Nov 2026), EC Today Co (98250 22222, follow-up = today), EC Week Co (98250 33333, ₹1,50,000, follow-up 24 Nov 2026), EC Later Co (98250 44444, follow-up 10 Dec 2026), EC NoStep Co (98250 55555, follow-up date cleared). As Test KAM (Mumbai): EC Mumbai Lead (98200 66666, ₹3,00,000, follow-up 18 Nov 2026). Deals in the default deal pipeline, owner Test BDM: EC Boiler AMC (TB Refinery Ltd, ₹10,00,000, stage Scope agreed 30%, next step 17 Nov 2026), EC Tank survey (TB Pharma LLP, ₹5,00,000, Quotation sent 50%, next step today), EC Crane check (TB Valves Pvt Ltd, ₹2,00,000, New deal 10%, no next step date); won: EC Won Oct (TB Refinery, ₹3,00,000, won 15 Oct 2026) and EC Won Apr (TB Pharma, ₹7,00,000, won 10 Apr 2026); lost: EC Lost Nov (TB Valves, ₹1,00,000, lost 5 Nov 2026, reason Price). Test KAM: EC Mumbai Deal (TB Steel Works, ₹4,00,000, Rate negotiation 70%, next step 20 Nov 2026). Accepted quotations: EC-Q-201 TB Refinery ₹5,00,000 + 18% = ₹5,90,000 (accepted 10 Nov 2026, PO EC-PO-0001 of 9 Nov) invoiced EC/INV/001 ₹2,95,000 dated and due 12 Nov 2026, unpaid; EC-Q-202 TB Pharma ₹7,80,000 + 18% = ₹9,20,400 (accepted 5 Oct 2026) invoiced EC/INV/002 ₹9,20,400 on 10 Oct, paid in full on 3 Nov 2026; EC-Q-203 TB Valves ₹1,00,000 + 18% = ₹1,18,000 (accepted 15 Nov 2026), not invoiced; EC-Q-204 TB Refinery ₹2,00,000 + 18% = ₹2,36,000 (accepted 1 Sep 2026) invoiced EC/INV/003 ₹1,18,000 on 5 Sep, paid 25 Sep; EC-Q-205 (Test KAM, Mumbai) TB Steel Works ₹4,00,000 + 18% = ₹4,72,000 (accepted 1 Nov 2026) invoiced EC/INV/005 ₹1,18,000 due 31 Oct, unpaid; EC-Q-206 TB Refinery ₹1,18,000 accepted 28 Mar 2026 (last year). A separate invoice EC/INV/004 TB Pharma ₹50,000 dated 15 Nov, due 22 Nov 2026. Contracts: CT-EC-101 TB Refinery ₹5,00,000 ends 1 Dec 2026; CT-EC-102 TB Pharma ₹2,50,000 ended 13 Nov 2026; CT-EC-103 TB Valves (no value) ends 20 Jan 2027; CT-EC-104 TB Refinery ₹12,00,000 ends 30 Apr 2027; CT-EC-105 TB Steel Works (Mumbai branch) ₹8,00,000 ends 15 Dec 2026; CT-EC-106 TB Pharma ₹90,000 ended 1 Oct 2026; CT-EC-107 TB Valves ₹60,000 ends today. Masters → Public holidays: 'EC national holiday' on Mon 23 Nov 2026 (no office = every office) and 'EC Mumbai holiday' on Tue 24 Nov 2026 for the Mumbai office only. Sales → Sales settings: weekly off = Sun only (the starting example).

EC-SALB-011VALIDATION

A 500-character subject — kept whole here, but cut to 255 characters on the MySQL test copy without a word

Who: Test BDM (login test.bdm) · Screen: /quote-new · Buttons: Create quote

Why it matters: A subject silently cut changes what the client reads on the PDF.

Before you start: Signed in as Test BDM.

#Do thisYou should seeOK?
1+ New quote, TB Refinery Ltd, paste a 500-character subject, Create quote.Expected: either the full 500 characters are kept, or the form says the limit (for example “Subject can be at most 255 characters”) before saving.☐

Fixed in update 329f18f or before: No length limit on the subject: on MySQL (VARCHAR(255), non-strict mode) a longer subject is cut silently (code reading; SQLite copy kept 500 characters).

Answered: Done: the subject box takes up to 255 letters and says so; a longer one is refused with its length instead of being cut.

Answered: Done: an accepted quotation is final and is not revised (the won order stays with Accounts).

A group-company contract under one rate quotation: only after the main contract, only for a linked company — and its value is ₹0 when left blank

Who: Test Finance (login test.finance) · Screen: /quote-add-contract · Buttons: Register a contract for another group company; Register group contract

Why it matters: One rate quotation may serve several group companies; each contract must carry a usable value.

EC-SALB-082NUMBERS

The quotation shows how its GST splits: CGST + SGST in the same state, IGST for another state, a note when a state is missing

Who: Test BDM (login test.bdm) · Screen: /quote · Buttons: (GST lines under the totals)

Why it matters: New 18 Nov 2026 (display only): the person reading the quotation sees the tax the invoice will charge. A wrong split here would contradict the invoice and the GST return.

Before you start: Settings → Tally export → "Our state" = Gujarat (on the copy it was empty until set — see step 1). TB Refinery Ltd with State Gujarat; TB Pharma LLP with State Maharashtra, no GSTIN. Draft EC quotations: A for TB Refinery 10 × ₹10,000 (₹1,00,000 + 18% = ₹1,18,000); B for TB Pharma, the same lines; C for TB Refinery 1 × ₹333.39 (GST 18% = ₹60.01); D GST 0%; E typed name "EC Typed Buyer Co" (not on the master).

#Do thisYou should seeOK?
1With "Our state" NOT set, open A.Under GST (18%) ₹18,000.00 a grey note. Expected: the note names what is missing ("our own state is not set in Settings"), not the client's state.☐
2Set Our state = Gujarat. Open A again."of which CGST 9% · same state (Gujarat) ₹9,000.00" and "of which SGST 9% ₹9,000.00". Total still ₹1,18,000.00 — nothing else changes.☐
3Open B (TB Pharma, Maharashtra)."of which IGST 18% · client in Maharashtra, another state ₹18,000.00".☐
4Open C (GST ₹60.01).CGST ₹30.01 + SGST ₹30.00 = ₹60.01 — the two halves add back to the GST, to the paisa.☐
5Give TB Pharma a Gujarat GSTIN (starts with 24) but leave State Maharashtra. Open B.CGST 9% + SGST 9% (₹9,000.00 + ₹9,000.00) — the GSTIN's state code wins over the typed state, as on the invoice.☐
6Open D (GST 0%) and E (no client on the master).No split line at all (nothing to split / no client record).☐
7Open the PDF of A.The PDF still shows one line "GST (18%) 18,000.00" — the split is a screen aid only.☐
8As Test KAM open a Mumbai-branch quotation for TB Steel Works Pvt Ltd (State Maharashtra).See the question: today it shows IGST 18% ("client in Maharashtra, another state") because "Our state" is one company-wide state.☐

Fixed in update 329f18f or before: When the company's own state (Settings → Tally export → Our state) is not set, every quotation's GST note says the split waits for "the client's state" even when the client's state is on record; on the test copy Our state is empty, so no CGST/SGST/IGST split is ever shown until it is set.

Answered: Your answer (20 Nov): yes, branch GSTIN. Done — a branch with its own GSTIN (Admin → Organisation) quotes and bills under it; Mumbai → Pune shows CGST + SGST.

#Do thisYou should seeOK?

2Directory → Clients (or Sales → Records → Client register): find EC Typed Buyer Co.Code in the office-year-name-number form, e.g. AHM-26-ECTYP-00004 (Ahmedabad, 2026, the first letters of the name, the next running number) — not the old GEN-…-0001 form.☐
3Open the quotation again.It now points at that client record; Accounts can register its contract.☐
